It opens close in and without the 1.3× raise. In the shared 3D view (`Route3DView`, so the route and area maps too) the credit is a slim strip in the corner instead of a white box over the map, and pins sit on the terrain from the first frame instead of after the first move. The wildfire and shade maps stay flat (3D cannot draw their overlay), as do the region thumbnails and the two map editors. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Protection rating right beside the grade everywhere; no season in the route header** (owner, 2026-11-18): the rating is printed right beside the grade wherever a grade appears (`displayGrade`, lib/grade.js: "5.8 PG13", including a route that stores it only as `prot_rating`), and in the route header as its own pill right after the grade pill (`splitProtection`). The header's discipline and grade pills are larger (`Pill` `lg`), and the strap under the route name no longer prints the season (still a ROUTE FACTS row). `check:bare` asserts the header half.
- **Friend activity says what the friend did** (owner 2026-11-18, audit): a Summit tick on a route now reads *Climbed Beckey Route*, not *Summited* (a route is climbed; whether a party topped out stays on the trip report and Recent outcomes). The three feeds (Home, Crew › Friends, All friends) each had their own verb table and disagreed; they now share `feedVerb` (`lib/feedVerb.js`), which also stops a conditions-only report, a boulder Project, an Attempt and a Turned around from printing as *Climbed*. Kudos on a real friend's log is keyed by the log (`kudosKey`): two logs of one route were one kudos, and the second asked to take back a kudos never sent. Home's Kudos can be taken back like the other two feeds, and Crew › Friends prints no *(null)* for a climb with no grade. → [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md)
- **Completed: every logged climb can be viewed and edited** (owner 2026-11-18): the website's Completed table has View / Edit under each climb's row (a row used to open only the climb, so your own trip report could not be read there), every card has a view button whether or not it has a write-up (*View log* on a sport, trad or top-rope send), and the opened report has *Edit* on your own log (`MyAscents`, `TripReport`). See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Editing a logged climb reads as an edit** (owner 2026-11-18, "check for bugs"): *Edit log* opened the form titled *Log this climb* with *✓ Log it* and *Delete trip report*, as if it were a new log. It now says *Edit your log* and *✓ Save changes*; the delete is *Delete this log* when there is no write-up, and a report's delete confirm says the logged climb is deleted with it (`LogAscent`); the toast says *Logged climb deleted* when there was no write-up. A conditions-only report has *View report* beside *Edit report*, and its opened report has *Edit*. The website's Completed table no longer cuts off Style and Stars: tighter cells and stars as a number fit it to the 669px column, and where the column is narrower (561px at 1280) it scrolls sideways (`MyAscents`). See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **A crew's organizer can hand the crew to another member** (owner 2026-11-18, 0474): the organizer was whoever created the crew, for good, so one who could no longer go could only *Leave crew*, which disbands it for everyone. Tapping a confirmed member on the crew card now offers the organizer *Make organizer*; after a confirm, `crew_hand_over` moves `crews.created_by`, the old organizer stays on as a member (and can then leave without disbanding), and the crew chat gets a line naming the new organizer. The organizer's Leave confirmation points at it. Real crews only. Detail: [logbook-crews-home](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Home's Pinned states card has no explainer line when signed in** (owner 2026-11-18): the sentence under the heading (*Pinning a state or province downloads its climbs to this device…*) is gone from `TodayTab`; the heading sits straight above the pins. A guest still reads the one line saying downloads are saved in this browser and that signing in keeps pins. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Route rows no longer print "#3 in this area"** (owner 2026-11-18, "do what you recommend"): the number was a crag climb's left-to-right position on the wall, but beside the stars it read as a ranking. The area list is still in wall order by default, and NEXT DOOR on the route page still names the climbs either side (`RouteRow`). See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Picking a crew lands on that crew's card, at its top** (owner 2026-11-18, "it's not quite spot on"): a crew row in the Menu opened the card and left the page where the list happened to sit, and the card's scroll offset (112px) was the website's header, so on a phone 53px of whatever sits above it showed over CREW OBJECTIVE. Every *open this crew* link (Menu › Your crews, the Crews tab's *Quick view*, the calendar, a DM's *Open crew*, an objective's crew) now goes through `openCrewCard`, the card rests 72px down on a phone and 124px on the website, and the last check closes any gap it measures. See [profile-and-settings.md](docs/features/profile-and-settings.md). **Same day, after driving the owner's own seven-crew account with real clicks:** the smooth glide is gone. It once never arrived, and twice the settle check fired mid-glide and threw the page ~2,400px PAST the crew before pulling it back. `scrollToCrewCard` now jumps to the card in one move, waits for the card by the clock (a slow chunk only makes it later), and holds it in place for 3s while the cards above finish loading; a scroll, tap or key ends it.
- **A free climb with an aid move is its free style first, aid second** (owner 2026-11-18, on Stripe Rock's Valhalla: "a sport climb, the second option is aid"): the import filed every route typed Aid under aid, ahead of its trad or sport type, so a bolted 5.8 A0 drew the aid glyph in search and read Aid before Sport. A route with a YDS grade and a free style now has that style as its discipline, with aid after it in the list; 668 routes moved (`scripts/oneoff/fix-free-discipline-before-aid.mjs`, rollback in `scripts/data/`), and both importers follow the rule. The 1,109 aid routes with a free grade but no style on record were then researched one by one (`scripts/data/aid-route-free-style-research.json`): 975 more moved (921 trad, 44 sport, 10 top rope), 110 are aid with no free style and stay aid, and 24 have no published record of how they are protected and stay aid. 21 of the moved rows had kept a difficulty number parsed from their aid grade (a 5.9 A1 stored as 1) and now carry the free grade's number; the move script recomputes it.
- **Overview's *View all reports & conditions* opens the Reports tab at its top, and holds it there** (owner 2026-11-18): the link now calls `scrollPageTop` (`lib/pinnedTop.js`) after switching tab, which puts the page back at the top until it stops moving (1s at most; the climber's own scroll wins). The single scroll every tab change already did was enough in Chrome and the report was not reproduced, so this is a precaution, not a measured fix. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Sub-area rows list their disciplines in words with counts, no colour dots** (owner 2026-11-18, "the discipline dots can be removed ... we need a better ui for listing the disciplines"): under each sub-area's name the phone's list reads *Alpine 820 · Trad 310 · Sport 110* instead of three dotted labels, so a row says how much of each there is. The Sort menu and its *Most sport / trad / …* orders are unchanged (`AreaChildrenList`). See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Website: the route page's tab row stays under the header, and a jump lands below it** (owner 2026-11-18, found looking at the hazard banner in the owner's Chrome): the height of the pinned header was read at ONE point, the exact centre of the window, and on the website a page-level box sits there, so the 112px header read as nothing. The Overview · Plan · … Safety row pinned itself at the very top, behind the header, and vanished on scroll; every jump to a section (the hazard banner, the jump chips, *Show less*) landed its heading under the header. `pinnedBottomAt` (`lib/pinnedTop.js`) now reads five points across the width; `RouteDetail` pins its tab row from it. Phones were unaffected.
- **Website search drop-downs: one open at a time, nothing under "Show more climbs"** (2026-11-18): a `WideSearch` list closed only on a mousedown elsewhere, so Tab from Areas to Routes left both open; a `focusin` outside the box now closes it too (a click on the list's own heading still does not). The Routes list's pinned "Show more climbs" stuck at `bottom: 0` inside the panel's 6px padding, so rows showed through beneath it; it now sticks at `bottom: -6` (`lib/DesktopShell.jsx`).
- **Home's Near me is a button, not a map** (owner 2026-11-18): phone Home showed the Climbs map inline with the five nearest areas under it; it is now a *Near me* box beside *Explore climbs*, in a new **Find** group (with *Find partners | Find crews* as the row below) above *Jump back in*'s four tiles, and it opens the Climbs map page centred on you. The compact form of `NearMePanel` is gone. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Climber-reported hazards are easier to find and open** (owner 2026-11-18, "audit that it works and improve ui"): the Overview banner is a button that says how many hazards climbers reported and carries its own *See the reports on Safety* action; it lands ON the climber-reported rows (`#climber-hazards`, ringed for two seconds), where it used to open Safety at the top with those rows under a *Show more* fold. KNOWN HAZARDS never folds now (`SafetyMatrix` folds only its seasonal and avalanche cards). Each reported hazard (`CRow`, also the Reports tab's consensus rows) is a boxed row with *View* / *Hide*, the reporters with dates in the climber's date format, and full-width *Still there* / *Gone now*. The banner counts by Safety's rule (two reports, or one in the last 45 days), so the two always agree. Reports kept private now feed the banner and Safety as they already fed the Reports tab, and the log-form chips are read one at a time: a "No ice" chip used to cancel the *Rockfall observed* chip beside it, "Avy: High" reached nothing, and "Hot/exposed" counted as an exposure hazard. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **The Safety tab no longer has a *Field checklists* card** (owner 2026-11-18, "Remove field checklist from safety tab"): the card between EMERGENCY & RESCUE and the float plan is gone from `RouteSafetyTab`. The checklists themselves were removed from the app on 2026-11-23 (see above). See [route page](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Desktop: the area page's search bar scrolls with the page** (owner 2026-11-18): *Search <area>* (Areas | Routes and the box) no longer pins under the header on the website, where the header's own search is always on screen. The phone still pins it, and the Route finder's bar is unchanged. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Near me rows print miles from YOU, or none** (owner 2026-11-18, "fix distance before location is allowed"): the list under the Near me map measured each row from the middle of the map view, so before any position it read *194 mi* from the middle of the country, and after a pan it was miles from a point the screen never named. `useFollowMe` (`lib/mapKit.jsx`) now returns `at`, where the dot is; `NearMePanel` prints a row's miles only from it and sorts nearest-to-you first, and with no position keeps the middle-of-the-view order with no distance.
- **A page naming a bundle the last deploy removed reloads itself** (owner 2026-11-18, "do what you recommend" on the live review): in the owner's Chrome the app sat on the loading splash for good. Pages lets a browser reuse the page for 10 minutes, two deploys landed inside that, and the reused page's entry bundle 404'd; `main.jsx`'s reload handler is inside that bundle. `index.html` now carries a classic inline script before the entry: a failed module script re-fetches the page past the HTTP cache and reloads once (30s guard, `climbmatch:boot-reload-at`); a second failure, or offline, makes the splash say *ClimbMatch could not load* (`check:chunk-reload`). Same PR: while the browser's location question is open the locate button reads *Allow location* (`locateLabel`, `useFollowMe`'s `asking`) and the Near me map draws that line muted instead of red.
- **Profile: CERTIFICATIONS & SKILLS has one edit control** (owner 2026-11-18, "we don’t need 2"): the card carried the header pencil and a large *Edit belay devices, certifications & skills* button at the bottom, both opening the same editor. The bottom button is gone; the pencil in the card header remains. `src/components/MeTab.tsx`.
- **A map waiting on the browser's location question says so** (owner 2026-11-18, "keep going" on the Near me audit): in the owner's Chrome a Near me tap sat on *Locating…* over the whole country, then said *Still looking for a GPS signal — step out from under trees or the wall* on a desktop PC whose location prompt had never been answered. `useFollowMe` (`lib/mapKit.jsx`) now reads the permission state: while the browser is still asking, every map says *Your browser is asking to use your location — choose Allow to see where you are*, from the press on; the GPS line is kept for a granted watch with no fix. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Grade spread: a stray scale gets no chip** (owner 2026-11-18): the second chip row (the scale) appeared on nearly every discipline, because a few climbs are filed on an odd scale (Washington's Sport offered *5.x · Class* for 3 climbs of 4,938). A scale holding under 5% of the picked discipline's graded climbs in that area is no longer a chip (`SCALE_MIN_SHARE` in `GradeSpread`, `check:area-page-honest`); those climbs stay in the type counts and the Route finder. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Grade spread: every discipline is its own chip** (owner 2026-11-18, "grade spread needs to have trad, sport, top rope be it's own discipline"): the chips over the chart were one per grading scale, so trad, sport and top rope drew together as *Rock*. They are now one per discipline with a graded climb under the area (*Trad · Sport · Top rope · Bouldering · Alpine · Ice · Mixed · Aid · Mountaineering · Scrambling*, only the ones it has), opening on the biggest of trad, sport and top rope; a discipline graded on two scales there (alpine: 5.x and Class) gets a second row to pick the scale, and a column opens the Route finder on that grade and that discipline (`GradeSpread`, `area_climb_stats.by_grade_disc` 0476, `check:area-page-honest`). See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Grade spread: a count up the left side** (owner 2026-11-18, "there should be a number on the y axis"): the chart now has a y axis of climb counts (0 to a round number at or above the tallest column, a faint line at each tick), so each discipline's columns say how many climbs they stand for (`GradeSpread`, `countAxis`).
- **Grade spread: pick the discipline** (owner 2026-11-18): an area graded on more than one scale shows a chip row over the chart (*Rock · Boulder · Ice · Mixed · Aid · Scramble · Snow*, only the ones it has), opening on Rock; the "276 of 385 list a 5.x grade · 106 on other scales" line under the chart is gone (`GradeSpread`, `check:area-page-honest`).
- **Route-page reorder: review fixes** (2026-11-18): the viewers' faces are back (in the header's *viewing now* cell); *No gear or rack listed* moved to the Plan tab above GEAR instead of vanishing; a scramble that stores a rappel keeps the rappel checklist; ON THIS BOULDER has a side gutter; the conditions card no longer says *no reports* twice; CROWDS is built only where it shows. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Signed-in Home no longer crashes after a crew join; three browser walks green again** (2026-11-18): the crew-join alerts (`_crewJoinNotifs`, 71035619) called `crewMemberById` 200 lines above its `useCallback`, so every signed-in account whose crew had a recent join got "This screen hit a bug — Cannot access 'crewMemberById' before initialization" instead of the app. `crewMemberUids`, `crewProfilesQ` and `crewMemberById` now sit above it in `ClimbMatch.jsx`. The test accounts (`scripts/lib/ui-fixture.mjs`) get a username, since without one (0420/0432) setup covered every screen; `check:signed-in` clicks the control, not its same-text wrapper, when opening the crew chat; and the trip plan's DAY tags and "N trip reports →" chip draw white on their blue boxes (`ItineraryView.tsx`). `check:signed-in`, `check:outage` and `check:blue-on-blue` pass.
- **A Near me tap finds you** (owner 2026-11-18, "when i click near me it doesn't show climbs or areas near me"): the *Near me* shortcut in the Climbs search opened the map centred on the climber only when the browser reported location as ALREADY granted; a phone that still had to ask stayed on the whole country until a second press on *Me*. The tap now requests the position itself (`autoLocate` in `NearMePanel`), and a second tap, already on the map, finds you again. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **The crag conditions score loads faster, and still appears once** (owner 2026-11-18, "why does conditions take a while to load?" / "I don’t want it to change though"): the score waits for the measured past rain so it never changes under the climber; that read now asks every 6-hour window at once (measured 0.56–0.71 s, was 1.0–2.0 s), keeps finished windows on the device so a return visit asks only for the newest, and starts when a crag route opens, not when the Conditions tab does. See `docs/features/conditions.md`.
- **A climber's match % is the same on a route's Partners tab as in the finder; Fitness specs reads in words** (owner check 2026-11-18): the route tab scored Avery 64% with *Ice*, the finder 70% with *Ice WI3*; the route tab now reads the same grades and verification. PARTNER REQUIREMENTS no longer prints raw field names: *packWeight* reads *Pack weight*. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Area page: one header card, and a crag lists its climbs first** (owner 2026-11-18): the name heads one card that also holds the path, Back, Area tree and Fire map (the same size as Save area); the separate Back/path bar is gone from the area page. On a crag or wall the Route finder row and the climbs list come straight after it, then the description, grade spread and classics. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Alpine, mountaineering and scrambling pages put the map with the way in** (owner audit 2026-11-18): Plan now runs drive, trailhead, access, the map and waypoints, approach, up and down, GEAR, timing, itinerary, camping, crowds, then the tracks; Overview shows TECHNICAL STATS first, folds *What to expect* into the conditions card and puts BETA before TOPO; Conditions shows the live forecast right under the flags card. Safety: trad on a peak gets the float plan, scrambles get the avalanche forecast and no rappel checklist. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **A "vouched for you" notification quotes the voucher's words, not raw JSON** (owner 2026-11-18, "fix it"): `vouches.reason` holds the whole vouch as JSON (route, ratings, skills, text…), and the notification printed the column as-is, so it read *Blake Tester vouched for you: "{"route":"Coleman–Deming…*. Every reader now parses it through one function, `parseVouchReason` in `ClimbMatchCore.jsx`; a vouch with no written words shows *vouched for you* alone.
- **One partner card for every climber** (owner 2026-11-18; #3416, #3508, #3518, #3532; migrations 0460, 0463, 0468): example and real climbers — on Partners and the route page's Partners tab — are all drawn by `RealClimberRow` with the old example card's look (no left bar): level and years, crew invites (one crew = one button, more under a chevron toggle), mutual friends, *N climbs · N vouches* (`partner_counts`, counted as the viewer's résumé counts them), the action and + Add friend inside the card. The List me box sits above the climbers, the Meanwhile list is gone, *Plan a session* names the climber's own listed place. See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md).
- **Climbs search: downloaded-only on the phone, two boxes on the website; Near me on Home** (owner 2026-11-18): the phone Climbs tab's search is `DownloadedSearch` — *Areas | Routes*, over only the states and climbs on this device (`deviceAreaSearch` / `deviceRouteSearch`, `lib/offline.js`), with *Manage areas* when nothing is downloaded. The website's top bar has an Areas box and a Routes box (`WideSearch only`). Phone Home had a **Near me** map (since replaced by a button, see above). A walk of many search variations then fixed: area search finds names CONTAINING the words ("yosemite", "joshua tree"), grade-only words skip the timing-out name search, a running search no longer flashes "No … match", held area-page rows dim, and the Near me map no longer fails cold. Migration 0459 indexes its read (peaks and crags by busiest and by lat/lng): the whole-US view reads ~6x fewer pages, its count ~25x fewer. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **A route's Partners tab no longer lists crews whose day has passed** (partner walk 2026-11-18): *Crews on this climb* offered a Sep 12 crew on Baker's North Ridge in November. It and *Sessions at <crag>* now drop a past-dated crew, as *Join a crew* already did. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Aid climbs list "Recent logged climbs" too, and a log with no stars stays a climb** (owner 2026-11-18, "do the rest"): aid joins sport, trad, top-rope and bouldering on the Plan tab. And the star rating is optional, but loading your logs filed every starless one with your conditions reports (a real Summit among the 14 live logs), and the bell said its climber "reported conditions on" it. One rule now decides, `isScoutRow` in `lib/tripReportRow.js`: tick *Conditions*, or no tick and no stars. → [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md)
- **Recent tracks & trip reports is one list** (owner 2026-11-18, "do what you recommend" on the section's audit): it and CLIMBERS' TRACKS under the map were two lists that disagreed ("See 1 older track" beside "No climber has shared a GPS track"), the per-row *Download standard route line* saved the route's own line named after the climber, and a climber's real recording could be drawn but never downloaded. Now each row is a party: stars, outcome and car-to-car on the row; opened, the report, *Read the full report*, and for a GPS track *Show on map* and *Download GPX* of their own recording. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Alerts audit: four alerts that were promised now arrive, and Unfinished business moved into Alerts** (owner 2026-11-18): *X vouched for you*, *X joined your <place> crew* (to the whole crew), *X reported conditions on <a climb you logged>* (the *Condition reports* switch muted nothing for a signed-in climber until now), and *Back in season: give <climb> another go* once per season for a climb you attempted and never sent. The Logbook card that listed those climbs, and the Logbook tab's amber *to finish* badge, are gone; the switch descriptions say what each one mutes. A GPS track the owner approves or rejects now tells its climber in Alerts (0469; it told nobody), and the notification switches are saved with the account, not the device. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **A multi-day trip is estimated as days** (owner 2026-11-18): the Planner's estimate added every day into one push, so 445 of 507 multi-day routes showed a red *After dark* or *Overnight*. It now shows the walk in, the summit day timed from camp (*Leave camp*), and the walk out; reds fell to 46 / 35 with a 4:00 AM *Leave camp* default. A summit day that walks out reads its summit leg one way (36 routes researched and corrected, Shasta and Forbidden among them). See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **One trip time per route, and no "incl."** (owner 2026-11-18): a route that publishes one car-to-car figure shows it once in the Planner, with a sentence saying it is not split into legs, instead of "incl." under Approach and Descent. 67 WA routes whose published total, legs, trip-plan days and summary disagreed were researched online and made to agree; 658 of 658 routes with two or more total figures now match (`audits/2026-11-18-trip-time-consistency.json`); 46 more multi-day routes whose summit day returns to camp were rewritten to "summit leg one way, the summit day includes the way back" (`audits/2026-11-18-trip-time-camp-summit-days.json`); a second, deeper pass applied 10 more, and the owner chose to fill the 39 whose times were blank or fragmentary with the researched low-confidence figures (95 in all); 8 whose times already add up stay as they were. **Stops:** a published total that counts rest runs past approach + summit + descent (15 routes, 0.25-1.7 hr); the PUBLISHED TIMES card now prints the difference as a *Stops* tile so its figures add up, and 4 rows whose day plan or rounding disagreed were corrected (Der Dihedral, Dragontail R3, Teneriffe, Tipping Point). Fairchild's day plan (28.5 hr) still trails its 31 hr legs; no timed report was found. **Blank day hours:** 35 multi-day plans whose day hours (and often legs) were blank were filled from research, and 4 routes with three legs but no total now show their sum; of the 10 held, 6 were then written on the owner's say-so (Jötnar's 182.5 hr elapsed-week total is now a 41 hr four-day plan; Challenger's legs follow its Easy Ridge day plan) and 4 stay as they are: Buckindy, Gilbert, Dana and Bacon (`audits/2026-11-18-trip-time-blank-days.json`). See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Suiattle River Road: the closure moved to milepost 12** (2026-11-18): the Forest Service's order in force since November 5, 2026 closes the road to vehicles from milepost 12, not ~milepost 4, so the walk to the Downey Creek Trailhead is about 8 miles, not 16, and the Miners Fire trail closure is no longer listed. 20 routes' road, access and trip-plan text now says so, their day hours now include the 8 walked miles each way (the earlier closure pass had added the miles but not the hours), and Frostbite Ridge no longer says to drive to the road's end (`audits/closure-distance/README.md`). The order closes the road to motor vehicles only, so walking and biking past the gate are allowed; it runs to October 1, 2028.
- **Your crews and Your groups left the website's right panel** (owner 2026-11-18): the rail now holds *Coming up*, *Notifications* and the ad slot; crews and groups are on their own tabs. See [app-shell-and-layout.md](docs/features/app-shell-and-layout.md).
- **Sport, trad and boulder pages read climb first** (owner audit 2026-11-18): a crag Overview now runs facts, description, TOPO, BETA, RACK and GEAR & SAFETY, difficulty, the pitches and descent, and then the map and the way in (drive, parking, access, walk-in, base); the whole getting-there block used to sit between the description and the rack. ON THIS BOULDER moved up into NEXT DOOR's place under the header, the Overview's second social row is gone on crags, and the crag Conditions tab shows the live forecast straight under the score. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Sport, trad, top-rope and boulder climbs list "Recent logged climbs"** (owner 2026-11-18): the Plan tab's *Recent tracks & trip reports* held only logs on these lines, each labelled "Trip report — no recorded track." with a GPX download of the route line. It is now headed *Recent logged climbs*, each row shows its tick, conditions-only scout reports and seed tracks stay out, and no GPX is offered. Other disciplines unchanged. → [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md)
- **Log form's Climbed with finds your friends and crew-mates** (owner audit 2026-11-18): typing a friend's or crew-mate's name showed nothing, because the search dropped anyone already offered as a chip; it now lists them first, labelled Friend / From your crews. A friend who hides their real name read "Climber" on their chip and was saved that way; it now shows their @handle. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **A climb-to-confirm answer can be changed; the Requests switch reaches every list** (owner 2026-11-18, "do what you recommend"): Crew › Requests has a *Climbs you answered* dropdown (the last 10, `LogTagAnswered`) where each says what you answered and offers the other one, so a mis-tapped *Wasn't me* no longer takes a partner's climb off Ranks for good. Turning off *Requests & vouches* now takes climbs to confirm and event invites out of Home's list and the rail as well as the bell; they still wait on Requests. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Climbs to confirm also live on Crew › Requests** (audit 2026-11-18): a tag row cleared or swiped away in Notifications took the only way to answer it with it, while Ranks still said *1 climb is waiting for you to confirm* and opened a bell that no longer listed it. Requests now leads with *Climbs to confirm* (every unanswered tag, the same `LogTagRow`), the Ranks line opens Requests, the Menu's Requests tile and the Crew badge count them, and an answer refreshes Ranks. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **"Based on limited info" counts where you climb only when it can be compared** (retest 2026-11-18): an alpinist with only mountain objectives and a crag climber with only listed areas share nothing either line can score; that pair now counts it as unknown, as its two "?" lines say. See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md).
- **The area page no longer jumps as it loads** (2026-11-18): the area search is drawn from the first paint on a state, region or range, and the grade spread holds its loaded height while counting, so the Route finder row moves 2-13px instead of ~150px (`AreaPage`, `GradeSpreadDisclosure`).
- **Area search under Climbs here by type on a phone** (owner 2026-11-18): every area page's *Search <area>* areas/routes box now sits right under the grade spread instead of after the facts and Partners cards, and stays pinned near the top as you scroll; and every section of the area page (grade spread, search, description, Route finder row, sub-areas, classics, photos, Getting there) is now its own spaced card on phone and website, not one shared sheet (`AreaPage`).
- **Six more route seasons agree with themselves** (owner "keep going" on the optimal-season audit, 2026-11-18): researched headers, windows and month calendars for Adams North Ridge, Adams Lava Glacier Headwall, Tye Peak (now its winter snow season), Honeymoon Route, Mount Lawson and Whatcom South Spur; `audit:optimal-season` reads a range that ends on a season word. Two held rows look misfiled (Adams *Wilson Glacier Headwall*, Goode *Northeast Face*). See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **5.10- / 5.10 / 5.10+ sort and filter inside 5.10** (owner 2026-11-18, "do what you recommend based on online research"; 0466): they were stored at the whole number, below 5.10a, so "5.10c and up" dropped every 5.10+. Now - = a, bare = b, + = c (10.25 / 10.5 / 10.75), the first letter of the usual a/b, b/c, c/d reading, as a slash grade is stored; 5.9± stays 9. 25,569 rows move, none out of its bar, so the grade charts are unchanged. The 17 *Bouldering in <place>* areas are kept: Mountain Project files them the same way, as a real area with its own access notes. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Route Partners tab: See all and List me land where they say** (owner 2026-11-18): *See all at <crag>* opened the finder reading *Searching United States* — the country dropdown renamed the crag it was handed — and it now opens AT *Searching <crag>*, with the dropdowns hidden until *Change*. *List me at <crag>* across a deploy reloaded onto the Climbs tab; the listing sheet is preloaded now, and a chunk reload reopens the route and sub-tab you were on. The crag and alpine explainer boxes and *Partners for crag climbs are found by area now* are gone, a signed-in crag tab drops the second finder button, and the selected sub-tab is no longer hidden under the › arrow. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **The match % no longer says "based on limited info" when nothing is missing** (browser pass 2026-11-18): every signed-in climber's own hiking pace was counted as unset, so a card with every line answered still said so, and one real gap was nearer to hiding the %. See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md).
- **A 5.10d is a 5.10 again** (owner 2026-11-18, "fix based on what you recommend"; 0464): a YDS d was stored as the next whole number (5.10d = 11, a bare 5.11's number), so the grade chart filed every 5.10d under 5.11 and a 5.10a–5.10d range took in every bare 5.11 — Leavenworth's 5.10 bar said 347 and opened 435. A d is now +0.9 in `lib/grade.js`, `picker_grade_num` and the finder's grade scale, and 0464 moves the 12,470 stored rows (selected exactly as the parser would). Also on the Route finder: the title box is quiet on a phone (the first climb starts ~50% down a 390px screen, was ~63%) and Area tree sits beside the path there; a peak's group heading shows its elevation in your units; on the website (a mouse) a form field is drawn at its own size, not forced to 16px — touch screens keep 16px so iOS never zooms. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **The area tree says *View* on a small phone, and the top search counts with commas** (owner 2026-11-18): under 380px every All areas tree row's button reads *View* (deep names were wrapping to three lines), and the top search's Places read *1,244 climbs*, not *1244*. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **Far less explainer text** (owner 2026-11-18): the fixed sentences that explained how to read a card are gone or cut to a few words — the TECHNICAL STATS note ("…is the climbing itself. Total ascent is the whole day…", "Round trip assumes…"), the commitment gloss, the leg card's pace and Loss/Regain lines, the trip plan's average-party caveat, the crag map caption, the forecast's hour/day/alert/snow/temperature notes, the Reports, Safety and Partners blurbs, and the same kind on the profile, résumé, route finder, calendar and smoke map. Honesty caveats stay, shortened (lower bounds, not-a-GPS-recording, no alert is not an all-clear, modelled temperatures). The camping intro and *Choosing a camp* were already removed 2026-11-14.
- **Route finder, second climber pass** (owner 2026-11-18): every state's page fired a whole-state route search with no words in it (`DbSearchSplit`), which timed out as an HTTP 500 on each visit; it now runs only for typed words on the Routes tab. Alpine, mountaineering and scrambling rows no longer print "#4 in this area", a crag's left-to-right position that reads as a ranking on a peak. Open: a grade bar and the list it opens disagree (Leavenworth's 5.10 bar says 347, the list 435), because 5.10d and a bare 5.11 are both stored as 11 — owner call.
- **Partners' name search no longer lists you** (compat audit 2026-11-18): searching your own name showed your own row with a match % against yourself (*84% match · based on limited info*). See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md).
- **The pitch editor holds still on the live site** (owner 2026-11-18, checked at 390px and 1440px): on the production build *Edit this pitch* landed 120px low, *Show all* threw the sheet to its bottom, and typing in a pitch moved it 15,000px, because the scroll measured from the wrong edge and the Route breakdown row printed every edited pitch in its two-word summary slot. The pitch now stays under the title bar on open, after *Show all* and while typing; every field row's summary is two lines at most; grown text boxes are no longer 2px short of their text app-wide. See [route-quality-control.md](docs/features/route-quality-control.md).
- **Add a climb fits the discipline; climbers can add OTHER DETAILS** (owner 2026-11-18, 0461): alpine is graded 5.x and mountaineering by class or snow grade, Commitment is I–VI, top-rope gets no descent, rappels or quickdraws, scrambles no rope/rack/5.x, aid its free grade, ice no first free ascent — in Add a climb and the edit sheet alike. Hidden fields are no longer submitted, a typed new crag opens *Add an area* instead of filing an unapprovable climb, and *Anything else?* rows (`routes.extra_facts`) print as an *Other details* facts row. Add an area can place a crag on a map. See [route-quality-control.md](docs/features/route-quality-control.md).
- **Route finder tidy-up** (owner 2026-11-18, "it looks all messy"): Back from a climb no longer leaves the finder's pinned search bar squeezed into a 0px strip, with the count line spilling down the left edge one word per line (`usePinnedBar` never pins a hidden bar, and measures again when it is shown); each wall heading names only the levels below the area being searched (*Icicle Creek*, not *Central-East Cascades, Wenatchee & Leavenworth › Leavenworth › Icicle Creek*); *Export CSV* says *40 of 703 loaded*; the website's inline *Find climbs here* form puts its button on its own row so *Left → right* is no longer cut to *Left → ri*, offers *All types*, and names the wall order *By boulder* / *By peak* where the list draws it that way.
- **The route edit sheet scrolls with a mouse wheel** (owner 2026-11-18, desktop): *Add or fix info* could not be scrolled on a desktop. Its scroll pane sat in a plain wrapper, so it never got a height and swallowed the wheel. The wrapper is now a flex column, so the pane scrolls and the Submit footer stays pinned. Desktop Notifications no longer leaves *See all notifications* under the window. `check:flex-scroll` now fails a `minHeight:0` pane whose parent is not flex.
- **No crag pin on a state's map** (owner 2026-11-18): the area page's map thumbnail (top left on the website) drew an orange *C* crag pin at the centre of every area, so Arkansas showed a crag in the middle of the state. It now frames a state, region, range or peak with no pin; only a crag or a wall keeps its *C* (`WalkInMap`'s new `center`).
- **COMPATIBILITY WITH YOU gives the same number from every door, and grades compare like with like** (compat audit 2026-11-18, 10 accounts): the grade term compared one climber's SPORT grade with the other's TRAD grade (a 5.11a sport / 5.10a trad leader read *Far apart* from a 5.9 trad leader); it now compares sport with sport or trad with trad, the closer when both set both, and names the kind. The profile now reads THEIR match objectives and listed areas itself, so opened from Friends, a chat or a crew it no longer says *Areas: not listed by both of you* and scores up to 10 points lower than from Partners. Two climbers with the same schedule (weekends only, say) now count fully on availability instead of an amber "partly". See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md).
- **The pitch editor keeps your place and shows the whole text** (owner 2026-11-18): *Show all N pitches* no longer pushes the pitch you were editing off the screen, and a pitch's belay, gear and notes boxes grow to their full text instead of cutting it mid-word on one line. See [route-quality-control.md](docs/features/route-quality-control.md).
- **The header season agrees with the route's own Optimal window** (owner note "Audit optimal season", 2026-11-18): `audit:optimal-season` compares the header strap, the *Optimal window* and its month calendar. A route with no `season` now heads its page with its own window before its area's (23 routes; 21 showed none); Park Glacier Headwall and Buckner N Face headers corrected to their spring/early-summer seasons; 4 windows that read as research notes rewritten. Seven contradictions held on thin evidence. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **A clear *View area* button** (owner 2026-11-18): every row that opens an area — the sub-area list, the All areas tree, the area searches and the top search's Places — ends in a solid blue *View area ›* (`ViewAreaPill`), so the tree's expand arrow and its open target no longer look alike. See [maps-and-area-browser.md](docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md).
- **A comment reaction shows its emoji alone** (owner 2026-11-18): in every Discussion, the React button showed your pick as emoji plus its word (*🤩 Psych*); it now shows the emoji only, the word moves to its accessible name, and the button always carries an add-reaction face (`ActionIcon` *smile-plus*) so it reads as a choice of more (`Comments`).
- **Editing one pitch shows one pitch, where you can see it** (owner 2026-11-18): *Edit this pitch* (and *Add belay, anchor & gear*) on a ROUTE BREAKDOWN row opened the fix sheet holding every pitch of the route, scrolled so the chosen pitch's header hid under the pinned title bar, in a sheet taller than the screen. It now shows that pitch only (*Show all N pitches* for the rest), lands it under the title bar, and the sheet fits the screen. See [route-quality-control.md](docs/features/route-quality-control.md).
- **Bouldering shows one grade** (owner 2026-11-18): the TECHNICAL STATS card no longer draws a *Crux grade* tile on a boulder problem, which repeated the grade in the header. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **The crux is WHERE, the grade is how hard** (owner 2026-11-18): the edit sheet and add-a-climb ask *Where the crux is* (text, every discipline) instead of a *Crux grade* that wrote into a column of descriptions; the page prints it as a Crux facts row. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Notifications: one row shape, and no phantom *1 new*** (owner 2026-11-18): Home's Notifications counted every alert as unread until the account's read-state landed, so it said *1 new* and then dropped to none; the count now waits for that read (a failed read still counts). A row that waits on you (*Confirm a day for North Buttress*, requests, unread chats) is now the same 55px card in the same `SwipeRow` as the notifications, so it archives or clears like them and no longer runs wider on the website.
- **An update reload is no longer cancelled by the phone Back helper** (2026-11-18, live): opening a popup whose file a deploy removed left the climber on the error card — the App unmounted under it and `lib/backButton.js` stepped history, which cancels a pending reload. `main.jsx` flags the reload first (`noteReloadPending`, `lib/chunkReload.js`) and the Back helper holds off.
- **A section a deploy removed says the app was updated** (2026-11-18, signed-in walk): the per-section card (`SectionErrorBoundary`) said *Something went wrong loading this section* with a Try Again that cannot work; it now shares `swallowedChunk` (`lib/chunkReload.js`) with `AppErrorBoundary` and shows *ClimbMatch was just updated* with Reload.
- **One editor per fact, and add-a-climb asks per type** (owner 2026-11-18): Pro tips folds into Beta, When to go and the conditions window into Best window, the rappel count note into the rappel table — each old field stays only where a route already stores one; add-a-climb stops asking a sport climb its first free / winter ascent. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **A screen a deploy removed no longer says it hit a bug** (2026-11-18, signed-in walk): opening a not-yet-loaded screen across a deploy (Menu › Listed areas) showed *This screen hit a bug (reading 'default')* — Vite hands `React.lazy` an empty module once main.jsx takes the reload. `AppErrorBoundary` now reads the reload marker and shows *This screen couldn't be downloaded … ClimbMatch was just updated* with Reload, and does not file it as an error (`check:chunk-reload`).
- **No ⋯ More on a crew card** (owner 2026-11-18): the card says how many more can join (*room for 2 more* / *crew full*); the size limit opens the Crew sheet (the friends picker stops at the room left); share link, Private / Listed and Leave / Remove are *Crew settings* at its foot; the safety plan opens from Ready up. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **A route map with no pins follows the discipline** (owner 2026-11-18, Golden Horn): a peak climb with no pins draws a Summit plus the trailhead its approach logistics records; a crag climb draws a Crag pin, not a Summit. 12 WA peak routes got the trailhead their own approach names (from a sibling route's pin); `audit:map-discipline` lists the 434 peak routes still without one. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **The route page names and can open what the edit sheet offers** (owner 2026-11-18): mountain routes show a TRIP SHAPE row (out and back, loop or point to point) above Full trip time; Published times, Climate & season and Partner requirements get a pencil; a boulder or top-rope header no longer shows an empty Approach or Gain cell; a long anchor or rock-quality note shows whole on a crag page. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Ranks looks the same signed in as signed out** (owner 2026-11-18: the signed-out board "looks cleaner"): a real row's points are drawn plain, with no dashed box or `›`, though tapping them still opens *How N points add up*; the ⚑ Report column is gone, and you report a climber from their profile (tap the row). See [ranks.md](docs/features/ranks.md).
- **A group can have its own avatar and cover photo** (owner 2026-11-18, 0454): the create/edit group form has a *Photos* section (`GroupPhotoFields`). The avatar replaces the drawn initial everywhere a group is listed (`groupMark`) and sits beside the name on the group's page; the cover replaces the colour band on Discover's cards (`groupBand`) and is a banner across the top of the group's page. Uploaded like a profile photo (`uploadGroupPhoto`), saved with the form; 0454's check refuses any address outside the app's own photo bucket. Without them, groups look as before. See [groups.md](docs/features/groups.md).
- **The browser tab names the route only while you are on it** (2026-11-18): App's `DocTitle` used the selected route's name on every tab, and `selRoute` is kept when you leave Climbs, so Crews read "Coleman–Deming Glacier – ClimbMatch". It now names the route only on Climbs.
- **Crew safety page: the questionnaire's Back moves into the top bar too** (2026-11-18, signed-in walk): on an account that has answered, *mark me safety-ready* pushed it below the top, so it stayed on the page under the bar's Back; it is now marked `data-cm-step-back` and taken over wherever it sits. See [app-shell-and-layout.md](docs/features/app-shell-and-layout.md).
- **A crag route's parking is the crag's pin** (owner 2026-11-18): *Add parking* on a sport, trad, top-rope, aid or boulder route opens the crag's own editor at *Parking pin*, so one edit fills every route there; the route edit sheet stops offering route waypoints on a crag unless the crag can't be edited or the route stores its own pin. See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **A Ranks row is no longer a button holding buttons** (owner 2026-11-18, "fix it"): each leaderboard row was one tappable element containing the points button and the ⚑ Report button, which a screen reader cannot use (axe `nested-interactive`, WCAG 4.1.2). Rank, avatar and name are now one real button opening the profile, with the points and Report buttons beside it; the row looks the same. See `docs/features/ranks.md`.
- **Top-bar Back: a step inside a screen goes first** (2026-11-18, app walkthrough): when a screen stacks two Backs at its top (a crew's safety page with its questionnaire open), both are hidden and the bar's Back — and the phone's — closes the questionnaire before leaving the crew (`findPageBacks`). See [app-shell-and-layout.md](docs/features/app-shell-and-layout.md).
- **The route edit sheet offers only what the discipline's page shows** (owner 2026-11-18, editing a trad route: Trip shape, Published times, Camping & bivy offered): one rule, `lib/fieldScope.js`, read by the edit sheet and the page sections. Crag routes lose the planner's fields; multi-pitch sport and trad get a DESCENT section; sheet labels match the page headings; add-a-climb asks the facts the sheet newly offers (`check:edit-scope`). See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Crews, log and vouch after a second climber walkthrough** (2026-11-18): a new log no longer pre-picks Summit and five stars; vouch lists climbs with that person first; a crewmate's tag of you turns the card's Log step into *Confirm their log*; a joined crew sorts first; *Remind all* waits a day; the meet-up skips a bare road name. See [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Partners and crews by PEAK; List me from Logbook › Saved** (owner 2026-11-18): 0456 lets a climber list themselves at a peak (alpine disciplines); Logbook › Saved shows areas above peaks, each row with a *List me* switch; Partners gains *A peak* (listed there + objectives on it) and Saved areas now searches saved peaks; Join a crew gains *By Peak*. See [partners.md](docs/features/partners.md), [logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Ranks › By area has a real area picker** (owner 2026-11-18: "By area for ranks needs a better ui"): the wrapping breadcrumb over up to 24 wrapping chips became a *Ranking in* card naming the area (its trail under it) with a **Change** button. What Change opens was rebuilt later the same day as a Choose-an-area page laid out like the Climbs tab (see Recent changes); the silent 24-sub-area cap stays gone, and a failed read of what is inside still says so. `src/components/RankAreaPicker.tsx`; see `docs/features/ranks.md`.
- **Float plans finished** (owner "do all", 2026-11-18): the route page's float-plan card opens the plan in place instead of jumping to the bottom of Crews (same scope and filing; `check:float-plan-persistence` 7 now requires that form to pass scope and who); the form's button reads *Save float plan*; an open crew plan in Crews › Float plans has *Open the crew ›*; crew messages spell *organizer* one way; the never-read `TRIP.ready` lists are gone.
- **Phone top bar: ☰ without the word Menu, and the screen's Back pinned beside it** (owner 2026-11-18): a screen's own ← Back moves into the sticky top bar (one Back, top left, stays on screen while scrolling); the website is unchanged (`lib/pageBack.js`). See [app-shell-and-layout.md](docs/features/app-shell-and-layout.md).
- **Route sections no longer hide themselves when you fold them** (owner 2026-11-18, trip plan *Hour by hour*): *Show less* lands the section below the app bar and sub-tab bar instead of behind them; a folded section that something scrolls from inside unfolds instead of showing its middle; the route page's jump chips land below the bars and follow a tab that is still loading (`lib/pinnedTop.js`). See [route-page.md](docs/features/route-page.md).
- **Groups: Recent posts from your groups folds after three** (owner 2026-11-18): For you shows three posts and a *Show N more posts* dropdown for the rest (`GroupsTab`). See [groups.md](docs/features/groups.md).
- **Sub-areas sort, A → Z by default; the area edit sheet's ✕ cannot scroll away** (owner 2026-11-18): every sub-area list (area page, area tree, pickers) opens A → Z; a Sort select adds Z → A, most / fewest climbs, most of each discipline (0455 `area_children_disc_counts`), objectives, nearest, highest and shortest walk-in, and phone rows show their top three disciplines. *Suggest an edit* keeps a sticky header, a Cancel + Send footer and Escape. See `docs/features/maps-and-area-browser.md`.
- **Float plans made signed out are no longer lost at sign-in** (owner "check it again", 2026-11-18): plans are keyed by account, so a guest's plan vanished from Crews › Float plans when they signed in ("No float plans on this device yet") while it sat on the phone. The list now says how many were made signed out and offers *Move to my account* (saved under the account before the guest copy is deleted; a guest crew plan becomes a trip plan) or *Not mine — delete* — never merged silently, so a shared phone does not hand over someone's emergency contact.
- **axe-core back on 4.14** (2026-11-18): the 4.10.3 pin and the Dependabot ignore from #3348 are lifted. #3354 gave every control a name that contains what it shows (WCAG 2.5.3) the same afternoon the pin went in, so the rule 4.14 gates, `label-content-name-mismatch`, has nothing left to report; a walk of this branch confirmed it before merge.
- **Browser walks run on main only, not on PR pushes** (owner 2026-11-14): `render-guards.yml` lost its `pull_request` trigger and keeps the 4-hour schedule and `workflow_dispatch`. Each PR push started 15 browser jobs against the production database; switched back on that afternoon after a day disabled by hand, the runner queue doubled and the database's health probe began failing within minutes (down 19:28–21:10 UTC; a correlation, not a proof). Disabling the workflow had also stopped the scheduled walk, so nothing walked a screen at all. A PR now gets `build`, the static guards and `zero-state`; a broken screen is caught on main within four hours, or at once with `gh workflow run render-guards.yml`. The workflow must be re-enabled in GitHub for the schedule to run. See `docs/guards/browser-walks.md`.
- **A crew whose climb didn't load no longer says the climb is gone** (owner 2026-11-14, during a database outage: both crews read "This crew's climb is no longer available"): `CrewCard` now reads App's route-read state (`RouteLookupCtx`) — loading shows *Loading this crew…*, a failed read shows *Couldn't load this crew's climb* with *Try again*, and only an answered read says the climb is no longer available. See `docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md`.
- **Float plan polish after a climber walkthrough** (owner 2026-11-18): the crew card's *All your float plans* link is styled again (`BTN_LINK` was spread uncalled, so it drew as a grey browser button; lint's new `no-restricted-syntax` rule now fails any uncalled palette style), a departure in the past or a hard return before leaving gets an amber note, the form's intro says what saving for a crew records (matching the Privacy Policy), and a list row no longer repeats its title as the crew name.
- **Partners tab consolidated after a climber walkthrough** (owner 2026-11-13): a **You card** above the pills holds how others find you (your listed areas + Edit, the *I'm free this weekend* switch — it was a pill among the filters but writes your own status — and one hint: listing turned off, no profile photo, or no gender; with no zip on file the zip form from #3306's point-of-use rule stays under the pills, in the same card look); the pill row is only who you're looking for, with **Distance as ONE pill** (Anywhere · Within N of <zip> · Near me now) and Sort moved into More filters, whose panel now opens under the row; **five modes** (My areas with *Where I'm listed* / *Saved areas* chips, Pick an area, My objectives, A route, Everyone); **every empty list is a card of one-tap next steps** (Search anywhere, the next radius, Pick an area, See everyone, Invite a climbing friend) over a *Meanwhile* list of real climbers; the fine print is one *ⓘ How matching and listing work* toggle; By Area says "in Utah" / "at Bad Bananas" and its list-me box sits under the results; and every box on the tab — the You card, the empty card, More filters, the real and example climber cards, the list-me box — now wears the crew card's look from one new `lib/cardStyles.js` (`CARD`, `EYEBROW`), which `CrewCard` reads too (owner: "make it consistent"). See `docs/features/partners.md`.
- **Float plan leftovers** (owner "go", 2026-11-18): saving from a crew's Safety screen files your crew float plan again (a stray arrow from #3369 made it a no-op; `check:float-plan-persistence` 8a now catches that shape); that tab starts filled in from the crew like the card; the route page's *File a float plan* also finds a crag-session crew; `SUPABASE_WORKDIR` lets `gen:schema` run from a fresh worktree, and SUPABASE_SCHEMA.sql is regenerated (0450). Live-tested in Chrome as Blake and Avery.
- **A crew's float plan opens on the crew card** (owner "go", 2026-11-18, float-plan audit step 3B): *Set float plan* in the card's Itinerary & float plan sheet now opens the form right there instead of jumping to Crews › Float plans at the bottom of the page; *All your float plans ›* still links to the list, which re-reads when the inline plan closes. Live-tested on desktop and at 390px.
- **Float plan form and list cleaned up** (owner "go", 2026-11-18, from the float-plan audit): required fields say *Required* and an incomplete form says what is *Still needed*; departure is prefilled from the crew day + meet time as a real date; your profile contact fills an empty contact field; list times read *Sat, Nov 21 6:00 PM* instead of raw ISO; empty drafts are no longer saved; Delete has *Undo*; a plan whose crew ended can be kept as a trip plan. Live-tested as Blake.
- **Partners › Find partners: the name search is a full-width bar** (owner 2026-11-14): the row under *Find partners / Join a crew* held the search title and a small *Name* pill that opened the name box. The box is now always shown and spans the column, and the title (*Climbers who climb where you do*, …) moved under the filter pills, right above the results it describes. See `docs/features/partners.md`.
- **Each crew member files their own float plan** (0450, owner 2026-11-18): new `crew_float_filings` (one row per member — date filed and the crew's return day, never the plan or a contact) replaces the organizer-only `crews.float_plan` stamp. The crew card's *✓ Your float plan* now says whether YOU filed, so an organizer's plan no longer turns every member green; crewmates are not shown yours. RLS proved in PGlite (`verify-0450`); `check:policy-claims` §5 re-aimed with an own-row test.
- **check:anniversary waits on the read its probe rides on** (2026-11-14): the probe log is prepended only after the account's own `climb_logs` read answers, a real read of the production database, and the check waited 20s for the notification line whatever that read was doing. On a slow database it went red with a message that blamed the feed (five times in a week, each green on a re-run). It now waits up to 60s for the read (`window.__annivLogsQ`, set by `scripts/anniversary.config.mjs`), and when nothing rendered and the read never answered it fails once, in the words `ci-db-inconclusive.mjs` knows, so CI probes the database and reports the run inconclusive only if the database is slow. A read that answered with no anniversary stays red.
- **axe-core held at 4.10.3** (2026-11-14): the grouped dependency bump (#3118) took axe-core to 4.14, which gates `label-content-name-mismatch` (experimental and off in 4.10), and `check:axe` then failed main on 215 nodes. `package.json` pins 4.10.3 and `.github/dependabot.yml` ignores axe-core until those accessible names get their own pass. The other seven packages of #3118 stay.
- **A crew member's float plan saves without the organizer error** (owner 2026-11-14: "it said it didn't save cause I wasn't the organizer"): `fileCrewFloatPlan` stamps the shared `crews.float_plan` only for the organizer (RLS 0036 refuses anyone else), toasts only after that write lands, and never stores the emergency contact there; a member is told *Float plan saved on this device*. Deleting a crew plan clears the organizer's stamp. Live-tested as member and organizer. Per-member filings and the inline crew-card plan are planned next.
- **Notifications: every row one size; every row swipes** (owner 2026-11-14: "notifications can be different sizes, make all the same size" / "swipe right to archive and left to delete for all of them"): the bell's rows are a fixed 57px (Home's card 55px, the website rail 53px), text clamps to two lines and ⋯ (or a tap, archived) shows it all. Friend requests became the same row (✓ Accept + ⋯ Decline/Archive) and swipe: left declines, right archives (`freq_<id>` in `dismissedNotifs`, listed under Archived). Climb-tag cards swipe too and keep their height. See docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md.
- **Groups: open on About, compact Join/Invite, only managers remove members, share an event, Discover scales** (owner 2026-11-13): a group always opens on *About* (closing it forgets the tab). The header's Join/Leave and *+ Invite*, and the event sheet's RSVP / Invite / Cancel, are compact pills instead of half-screen slabs. The remove-member ✕ shows only to the owner and moderators, never on yourself or the owner (the seed *Wasatch Trad Collective* listed you as a moderator, so joining it handed you the ✕). A public group's event sheet has *Share this event*: Text, Email, Facebook (the message is copied to paste into the post, for Facebook groups), Copy link and the phone's own share sheet; the link `?group=<id>&event=<id>` opens the event for a member and the group's About with a Join prompt for anyone else. Discover shows at most 6 state chips (yours, then the busiest) and one *State* list of every state (no per-town options), and pages its results 20 at a time. App-wide, other oversized secondary buttons became compact centred pills sized to their label (crew card View plan / Collapse / Safety plan / Leave crew, Edit résumé, Me › Edit profile, Vouch, Request to join crew, + Create an event, Manage areas, Manage offline areas, Add a climb, Show more, Replay the walkthrough); the Filters bar, the Discover mode toggle and Climbed it keep their width with less padding.
- **Partners: no profile photo needed to be listed** (owner 2026-11-14: "i dont want people to have to have a profile picture to be shown to other climbers"): `lib/PartnerSearch.jsx` no longer drops real climbers without a photo from the Browse / Near / Objectives / areas / saved lists (they show the silhouette avatar), and the "You aren’t shown to other climbers here until you add a profile photo." hint is gone. Sending a request no longer asks for a photo either (`sendReq` in ClimbMatch.jsx; owner: "we don't need a photo for anything related to finding partners"). The setup photo step and Home/Edit-profile photo prompts stay as optional, skippable suggestions.
- **Partners: no profile photo needed to be listed** (owner 2026-11-14: "i dont want people to have to have a profile picture to be shown to other climbers"): `lib/PartnerSearch.jsx` no longer drops real climbers without a photo from the Browse / Near / Objectives / areas / saved lists (they show the silhouette avatar), and the "You aren’t shown to other climbers here until you add a profile photo." hint is gone. Sending a request still asks for a photo first (`sendReq` in ClimbMatch.jsx), unchanged.
- **Area charts answer from a cold cache: stored subtree totals** (owner 2026-11-14: "check on california for me" → "do what you recommend"): migrations 0440 + 0447 add `area_subtree_rollup` (the routes strictly below each area, by path, in 0421's buckets), kept by `trg_area_route_rollup_up` (an area's own counts carried up its path) and `trg_areas_path_moves_rollup` (a moved area's own counts leave its old ancestors and join its new ones). `area_climb_stats` and `area_children_grade_counts` read one area's rows instead of summing 12,532 areas for California, which timed out cold (HTTP 500 at 3.2 s, then 2.1 s) after a database slowdown. 0440 builds and fills the store (production fills it one state per transaction with `area_subtree_rebuild`: the first one-shot apply hit the management API statement timeout and rolled back); 0447 points the charts at it. Same output.
- **Profile: no more safety score; the Safety tab is now Skills** (owner 2026-11-14): the made-up "Overall safety score — NN/100" bar is gone from every profile (your own and other climbers'), along with `safetyScore` in the seed climbers. The tab is renamed **Skills** and leads with the skills the climber added in Edit profile → Skills (with when they last practiced each), then belay devices and certifications; an empty list reads "None listed" (on your own profile: "add yours in Edit profile"). `src/components/FullProfile.tsx`.
- **No "Add the first photo" box in the website route header** (owner 2026-11-12: "remove that, they can add photos in the photos tab"): at 1024px+ `RouteDetail.jsx` shows the header photo tile only when the climb has a photo; with none (or a failed photo read) the facts take the full header width. Adding photos stays in the Photos tab; phones are unchanged.
- **Website: "Show details" on ACCESS & REGULATIONS opens again** (owner 2026-11-14: "the dropdown doesn't happen"): the desktop rule that sizes `width: 100%` buttons to their label (`lib/wideLayout.js`) also shrank the fold's invisible full-row toggle to 180px, so the pill on the right sat outside it and a click did nothing. Absolutely-positioned overlay buttons are now exempt; the route header's "See all photos" overlay had the same fault. Phone was never affected.
- **Research ledger: confirmed route facts lock (0437)** (owner 2026-11-14: "index when you are highly confident of a field being correct so that the next agent or session does not try to research or change that field"): `route_field_evidence` holds each researched field’s value, sources, method and lock level; `enrich:apply` refuses any write that changes a locked field (3+ independent sources to overturn) and stores evidence only after a verified write; a lock makes the field High for climbers. Rules in `.claude/skills/route-research/`, mechanics in docs/codebase/research-ledger.md, held by `check:research-locks`.
- **Closing Finish setting up sticks** (owner 2026-11-14: "why does finish setting up sometimes show and sometimes doesn't?"): its ✕ lasted only until the next reload, so the card came back. The dismissal is now remembered on this device for each account (`lib/homeDismiss.js`); the bell still reminds you while the profile is incomplete. *Set up your climbing profile* (onboarding is required) still returns on reload.
- **Crew cards after a climber walkthrough** (owner 2026-11-14, #3377): a *Your friends* picker invites several at once; You / In / Invited chips are one size; the step sheet's Done and Next never move; risk tolerance comes from the crew's own answers in a Safety & risk panel; a late joiner is asked about the agreed day instead of reopening it. See [docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md](docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md).
- **Climbing-days grid; Completed as a sortable table on the website** (competitor review batch B, item 18, owner 2026-11-04): a 52-week grid of the days you logged a climb sits above Completed, in Year in Climbing and on a real climber's public profile; on the website Completed is a sortable table (Table / Cards toggle). Phone cards unchanged. See `docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md`.
- **Joining a group sticks** (owner 2026-11-14: "Does joining a group persist? It didn't seem to"): the my-groups hydration REPLACED `joinedGroups` with the server's list, so joining any real group (which refetches it) or a reload dropped every example group you had joined; it now keeps example ids, and `lib/seedGroupJoins.js` remembers example-group joins/leaves per account on this device. The hydration also moved BELOW the sign-in reset, which used to wipe a cached my-groups result on sign-out/sign-in so real groups read "Join" again. Real groups were already persisted (`group_members` row / `join_group()`).
- **Setup and sign-up redesigned to look finished** (owner 2026-11-14: "make it look professional and complete with good ui and ux", after a walkthrough as a new climber and research into how Strava, KAYA, Mountain Project, Bumble BFF, Hinge and others onboard): setup (`lib/Onboarding.jsx`) is a centred card with a step rail on desktop and a cleaner full-screen sheet on phones, with "Step N of 6", one primary button per step and *Skip for now* as a link. The username step suggests clean handles and checks availability as you type (`isUsernameFree`); the home step looks a town up on Enter (`searchTowns`); grade boxes appear only for picked disciplines; *About you* adds experience level (Partners filters by it); and setup ends on a *You're all set* screen with next steps instead of a toast. Sign-up (`lib/AuthModal.jsx`) opens on *Create account* for a new visitor, hides the Apple/Google buttons until one works, asks the password once with Show/Hide (the email still twice) and suggests a fix for a mistyped email domain.
- **Route finder: area path on every result, Filters fit the discipline, Area tree button** (owner 2026-11-14): results show each wall’s area line (e.g. City of Rocks › Circle Creek Overlook › wall) via `useAreaTrailsByIds` (lib/db.js, offline twin in lib/offline.js) and in the CSV. The Filters sheet shows a filter only while a ticked discipline uses it (`FILTER_DISCS` in RouteFinderPanel.tsx: gain/round trip/peak elevation/prominence for alpine/mountaineering/scrambling; no pitches for bouldering/top rope/mountaineering/scrambling; no length for bouldering/mountaineering/scrambling) and clears a hidden filter’s value; alpine/mountaineering/scrambling sort By peak instead of by wall. Filters sits beside the search box; Compact rows / Export CSV moved out of the pinned bar. A labelled *Area tree* button sits beside Back on every Climbs screen (phone too).
- **Setup is required for an account; usernames lock once chosen (0432)** (owner 2026-11-14: "You shouldn't be able to switch your username once you've chosen it… let's audit what we should require"): a signed-in account's setup (`lib/Onboarding.jsx`) now has a required **username** step (saved on Continue), a required **home area**, and a required **grade** when a picked discipline has a grade ladder (rock, bouldering, ice, mixed, aid). It cannot be skipped (Sign out is the way out) and reopens on every load until done; photo, gender and alerts stay optional and a guest can still skip everything. **0432** locks the username in the database: one change in the first 7 days, then refused (`profiles_username_lock`; support override `set local climbmatch.username_override='on'`), plus a case-insensitive unique index. Edit profile shows the lock and makes the field read-only once locked. Asked where it matters instead of at signup: **Partners** lists real climbers only once a zip is on file (inline zip card; example profiles untouched), real climbers without a photo are not listed, and **sending a request** to a real climber asks for a photo first. The Terms notice for an account with no acceptance on file (every Google/Apple sign-up) now says ClimbMatch is 18+ and reads "I'm 18 or older — I accept". Emergency contact is not required (owner).
- **Guide feature removed** (owner 2026-11-14: "remove everything about guide, hiring a guide, applying to be a guide"): Partners is now Find partners / Join a crew; the guide directory, inquiries, reviews, guide application, guide dashboard, Menu/Settings guide entries, the admin Guide applications queue, the inquiry badge, and the guide sections of Help, Terms and Privacy are gone (`lib/DbGuides`, `DbGuideApply`, `DbGuideDashboard`, `GuideApplications` and their `lib/db.js` helpers deleted). 0445 then dropped the guide tables (`guide_profiles`, `guide_credentials`, `guide_documents`, `inquiries`, `reviews`, `cert_track_disciplines`) and their functions; the `guide-documents` bucket is deleted via the Storage API and `delete-account` no longer lists it. Guiding certifications stay on a climber's résumé (owner).
- **Save vs List me, said where you tap** (owner 2026-11-14: "whats the difference between saved areas and my areas?"): an area page's *Partners here* card now says listing makes you findable by climbers searching there while saving the area only bookmarks it for you, and the signed-in Save toast reads *Saved — only you see it, in Logbook › Saved*. See `docs/features/partners.md`.
- **Last practiced per skill, and practice vouches** (owner 2026-11-14, "do what you recommend", after PR #3331): 0430 adds `profiles.skill_practiced`; Edit profile asks an optional month per ticked skill, the profile shows it, and your own profile suggests a refresher after 12 months (`lib/skillPractice.js`). A vouch can record *We practiced these together* on a date — a practice record, not a competence rating ([profile](docs/features/profile-and-settings.md)).
- **The phone's Back button steps back inside the app** (owner 2026-11-14, step 3 of the swipe plan): Android's Back and Safari's edge swipe used to close the app from anywhere (no history entries). `lib/backButton.js` keeps ONE same-URL history entry while there is somewhere to go back to; Back taps the top layer's own ✕ / ← Back (`findBackControl` in `lib/dialogA11y.js`: top full-screen layer, a dismiss control at its top, a backdrop for a dialog with none, a cover with no way out waits), else Home from another section's main screen, else leaves the app on the first press. Tabs are not history. Phone only. Independent review (forward = not back, rotate/resize, reload, double Back, click-catcher layers) + 32 phone checks.
- **"You are here" on every map is steadier and lands nearer the truth** (owner 2026-11-14: "where you are on a map needs to be more accurate"): the dot is now a running estimate of the phone's readings (`lib/fixFilter.js` `smoothFix`) rather than the latest raw one, so it stops hopping 5-30 m while you stand at a route and settles closer; it still keeps up walking and resets on a drive. A coarse first fix (wifi/tower, over 100 m) frames its ring instead of zooming to street level on the wrong spot; the map zooms in when GPS lands. A late, older reading no longer pulls the dot back. Guard: `check:fix-filter`.
- **Post an open crew, polished** (owner 2026-11-14: "polish it"): the website's quick form on the Crews tab has labelled *Climb* and *Day* fields, climbs grouped by saved list, weekend day chips with *Other day…* for the date picker (no bare mm/dd/yyyy box), and a "Posts X on Sat, Nov 21" line before *Post crew*. See `docs/features/logbook-crews-home.md`.
- **Classic climbs on the area page read only classic routes** (owner 2026-11-14: "do this part now — the other area-page sections that count across the whole area"): migration 0423 adds two partial indexes (`routes_classic_area_idx`, `routes_state_classics_area_idx`) and rewrites `area_classics` as plpgsql with one literal query per case, so a state reads its ~100 top climbs instead of every route (California timed out at 3.2 s) and a crag reads its classics only (Red River Gorge: 1,066 of 3,918 rows, was 659 ms). Same output. Latest reports (87-183 ms) and the photo feed (87-140 ms) were measured and left alone: one round trip each while climb_logs and topos are small.
- **Owner Review desk: flags and suggested edits as one AI-checked table, off the Menu** (owner 2026-11-14: "I as the owner need a better way to have ai check those flagged fields in a database or table way … not part of the app in the menu"): new standalone page `review.html` (own Vite build, admin only, unlinked) lists every held field edit and open route flag with the nightly AI checker's verdict, value and quoted sources (`review_ai_verdicts`, migration 0436; `scripts/review-queue-export.mjs` → `.claude/workflows/review-flags-nightly.js` → `scripts/review-verdicts-write.mjs`). The Menu's Route edits + Flagged details tiles are gone; see docs/features/route-quality-control.md.
- **Climbs open sooner; area pages ask less** (owner 2026-11-14: "anything else we can speed up?" → "do it"): `lib/routePrefetch.js` starts a climb's full-row read on touch/press (and after a 90 ms hover, or keyboard focus) of a thin row marked `data-route-id` (RouteRow, ClassicsList), so the open finds it loaded. `useScopedWishlistRoutes` is keyed on the saved ids only and filters per area in `select` (it re-read every saved climb on each area visit). Eleven catalog hooks hold data 15 min (`CATALOG_STALE_MS`), not the 60 s default; stars/stats/photos keep the default. Migration 0428 adds `latest_refs` to `area_latest_reports` so Latest from this area reads reports, climbs and authors together (2 round trips, was 3).
- **Skill guides and field checklists** (owner 2026-11-14, "do what you recommend" after the technique research, PR #3331): 30 skills get a card (what it is, when you need it, what goes wrong per the accident record, links out to Petzl / VDiff / Alpine Savvy / AAI / AAC / Ortovox, and courses, Washington first) with a visible "information, not instruction" line; no in-house rescue walkthroughs and nothing copied. Tap a skill on a profile or in a route's *Your skills*. The field checklists that shipped with them were removed from the app on 2026-11-23 (owner). `lib/skillGuides.js`, `SkillGuideSheet`, `SkillGuideLink` ([route page](docs/features/route-page.md)).
- **Leg cards show the downhill on the way up and the uphill on the way back** (owner 2026-11-14: "mt goode northeast buttress has a pretty big descent on the approach, but the stats don't show that"): TRAILHEAD TO SUMMIT prints Loss, SUMMIT TO TRAILHEAD / DESCENT print Regain, CAR TO CAR both — from PUBLISHED figures only (owner, same day: "i dont want to have to do any of our own math, all the information should be online"): migration 0449 adds `routes.approach_loss_ft` / `return_gain_ft`, written by research with sources in `route_field_evidence`, read by `publishedLegClimb()` (lib/tripLegs.js). PR #3352's track measurement was removed. A loop's whole-outing gain is no longer printed as the summit leg's (Goode read ↑10,700); the note states it. Scrambling now prints its Gain. Audit of all 6,628 alpine/mountaineering/scrambling routes in research-data/gain-loss-audit-2026-11-14/ (5,752 have no gain at all; 211 tracks lack heights). Guard: check:leg-loss-regain.
- **A peak list ticks only from a route that reaches the summit** (owner 2026-11-14: "they can't log a bouldering route and get the tick for climbing Castleton Tower"): all 1,496 routes able to tick a list peak were reviewed (`research-data/list-summit-audit/audit.json`); 370 that stop short (a V1 at Castleton's base, Pikes Peak and Mt Blue Sky flank crags, Quandary/Lindsey gulch ice, Temple Crag aretes, start variations, satellite spires, ski descents) no longer tick the peak or wear the list chip (`lib/listSummit.js`), bouldering and top-rope never do, and 43 crag sub-areas were detached from their peaks. `check:list-summit-audit` (in build) fails a new list or peak that was not audited.
- **Crew cards: a guided journey, one step per sheet** (owner 2026-11-14: "we are just providing everything at once"; owner chose guided, not gated): `CrewCard` is now a compact card (avatars, a 7-dot Crew → Day → Meet-up → Ready → Climb → Log → Vouch strip from `crewStage`, ONE next-step button, step rows that each open `CrewStepSheet`) instead of a ~3,500px "View plan" expand.
